## 2026-06-18 — DECORRELATION-PROXY EXPERIMENT → VERDICT (D186): decorrelation belongs at ASSEMBLY (Crucible), not generation. The generation layer owns QUALITY + directional variety; the free structural proxy is too weak and a per-recipe map is NOT warranted. RESEARCH — no production-loop change.

**Operator: *"how do we use an ML model for strategy generation"* → tested Crucible's `FORGE_generation_model_plan.md` §2 `quality × decorrelation` objective. Full record: [[D186]].**
- **Experiment:** `scripts/decorrelation_proxy_alignment.py` (offline, deterministic) tests whether a FREE structural proxy (Jaccard over indicator-id sets) predicts Crucible's realized PnL correlation. Sample: `decorrelation_proxy_sample_20260618T235737Z.json` (1000 pairs / 70 configs / 810 broad×broad / 100% resolved). PREFLIGHT caught `content_key` is degenerate (saturates at distance 1.0) → switched to indicator-id distance.
- **Findings (broad×broad n=810, calm→stress):** decorrelation is **ABUNDANT** (mean |corr| 0.098→0.124) — broad-xsect components already decorrelate regardless of structure; the proxy is **WEAK** and only in directional-indicator distance (Spearman −0.195 calm / **−0.228 stress**; regime-gate = noise −0.02); mild stress re-coupling (+0.026); residual variance is **per-pair, not per-recipe**.
- **Verdict:** decorrelation → **ASSEMBLY** (Crucible has real pairwise corr; the §8.2 joint objective lives there). **No Forge decorrelation-yield axis** (weak proxy, wrong granularity). **Per-recipe map NOT warranted** — recommend a cheap directional-diversity guardrail at assembly; revisit only if the correlated tail (~12–16%, growing under stress) proves binding.
- **Generation layer = QUALITY + varied menu:** mint strong component-grade legs toward the WF-median CENTER gate (generation-layer ML = a QUALITY model, NOT a decorrelation model); keep directional/hypothesis variety as a floor; push the quality frontier into under-served hypotheses (a GRAMMAR problem, operator-gated).
- **Side-finding:** Forge cohort (`combiner.type`) vs Crucible cohort (execution breadth) diverge for 8/70 configs (confluence-on-universe tagged `xsect`) — flagged for [[D182]]; verdict robust to the definition (tool segments by the sample's tags).
- **Caveat / scope:** n=70 good broad components — signs/stress-strengthening robust, magnitudes maybe attenuated. **No `src/`/grammar/feedback change — not a deploy.** Crucible writeback pending operator go.

## 2026-06-13 — D137 BUILT: §7.3 stall guard (Q38 design shipped) — decision-clock staleness + work-pending predicate in `check_rate_limit`; full suite 1,563/0, mypy 0/89; SERVICE-INERT until next ritual restart

**Operator: "let's build the stall-guard" on the APPROVED Q38 design (`docs/proposals/limiter-stall-guard.md`). Built TDD (RED-first): predicate `stall_blocked ⇔ export readable ∧ ∃ submitted row with submitted_at > max(decided_at) ∧ ≥ T old` (T=3h, `submission.stall_after_seconds: 10800`). The fix for the 06-10 wedge — Crucible's gate quiet 18h while its export stayed fresh-by-mtime, the completion-fraction signal stayed clear (oldest batch 199/200), ~13k configs into a dead gate. Stateless (one fresh decision clears it next poll — the D110 anti-latch lesson made structural) + deadlock-immune (a clock left stale by Forge's OWN quiet has no submission postdating it → guard silent). Full record: [[D137]].**

- **Shipped as designed (all four §8 options):** decision-clock predicate / T=3h / direct-enforce / extend `check_rate_limit`+`RateLimitStatus`. New fields `stall_blocked` / `last_decided_at` / `stall_pending_count`; distinct journal line `blocked: crucible stalled — no decisions since <ts> (<X.X>h); <N> configs pending ≥3h` (kept separate from the benign `prev batch N% gated`). One note: the line's `<N>` makes the predicate a COUNT (not bare EXISTS) — same single query.
- **Rollout posture (deliberate, vs H-4):** code-layer default + no-config fallback = **0 (OFF)** because the guard reads the wall clock (a stale test DB would false-trip); **production opts in via `config/forge.yaml: stall_after_seconds: 10800`**. Keeps all 14 existing rate-limiter tests + every cmd_run/_run_one_iteration test byte-identical; "guard-off equivalence" is the literal invariant.
- **Backtest (design §5, full verdicts history):** 2/2 true stalls fire (05-30, 06-11), 0/2 false (healthy-slow 06-04, Forge-quiet 06-07 migration silent). Would've caught the 06-10 wedge at +3h, saving ~10,800 of 13k dead submissions.
- **Verification:** 12 unit (8 truth-table + 4 §5 replay) + 3 invariants (deadlock-immunity Hypothesis property, stateless-recovery, guard-off-equivalence) + wiring/model tests. **Full suite 1,563/0; mypy 0/89; ruff clean.** tz gotcha handled in fixtures (synthetic crucible DB not UTC-pinned → store naive-UTC for identity round-trip; production reads tz-aware export JSON, unaffected).
- **SERVICE-INERT** — the running daemon won't reload code/config until a restart; activates at the next D104 ritual restart (no urgency — runner recovered; the next stall is what this buys down). Docs updated same commit (MANPAGE knob + §7.3 note, HOW-TO troubleshooting, investigate-live journal signal). **Next: this rides the next operator-gated restart; until then nothing changes live.**

Stateless ⇒ cannot latch (the D110 lesson); work-pending clause ⇒ deadlock-immune when the staleness is OUR quiet. D110's frozen watermark keeps the witness row alive for the stall's whole duration.
- **Calibration (verdicts table, full history):** healthy gaps p50 7 s / p99 4.7 min / max 65 min; all four >2 h gaps classified — detector at 3 h fires on both true stalls (05-30 17.1 h / 10 k subs; 06-10 18.1 h / 13 k subs), silent on healthy-slow 06-04 and the Forge-quiet 06-07 migration gap. **Backtest 2/2 true, 0 false.** At 3 h, the 06-10 wedge would have been caught at 02:55Z — 10,800 of 13,000 dead submissions avoided.
- **§8 decisions APPROVED (all recommended):** decision-clock predicate; T = 3 h (`submission.stall_after_seconds: 10800`, 0 = disabled); direct enforce riding the next D104 ritual restart (the full-history backtest is the shadow evidence); extend `check_rate_limit`/`RateLimitStatus` (avoids a third ~50 MB export parse and a second main.py seam).

## 2026-06-10 — D132: learned-ranker design PROPOSED (`docs/proposals/learned-ranker.md`) — calibrated P(component|features) upgrading §6.2's prior_promotion_proximity slot, shadow-first (F1 dataset → F2 shadow+eval → F3 wiring+per-arm floor); docs-only, awaiting operator decisions §8

**Origin: operator brainstorm ("incorporate learning prediction models for generation?") → "lets do the sketch out for it and design". No code/grammar/weights/service change — the proposal stops at three operator gates.**

- **The case:** §6.2's only learning term (prior_promotion_proximity, weight 0.10) is non-discriminating — `promoted_patterns` empty, 0 promotions all-time; and per-cell weight counters can't generalize to new arms (live symptom: iv_minus_rv 2/600 submitted vs ~4% emission at the 18:53Z baseline). A regularized logistic regression over config-structural features (pure-Python Newton–IRLS, **zero new deps** — numpy/sklearn verified absent) gives the ranker an outcome gradient AND a feature-based prior for never-seen arms. Deterministic by construction (convex, zero-init, no RNG — nothing to seed); hard-rule table in the doc (#5/#6 clean).
- **Honesty posture:** labels = component/promote under the D128 honesty predicate (imported, not copied); training rows hard-cut at the composite clean-era boundary **2026-06-10T17:17:13Z**; any future era boundary auto-obsoletes the model (era guard refuses + falls back to the Jaccard path, D076 two-mode precedent). Artifact = append-only JSON w/ coefficients by feature name (grammar_archive analog).
- **The coupling rule:** F3 live wiring ships ONLY with the per-arm exploration floor (young arm = (role, indicator_id) < 25 honest verdicts → ≤2 reserved slots/arm, ≤10% batch, D103 diversifier-floor precedent) — otherwise the model's training data becomes conditioned on its own choices (closed-loop selection bias, the design's central risk).
- **Also closes a telemetry gap:** F2's `shadow_scores` table persists the incumbent composite score (never stored today) — we currently can't measure whether the existing ranker ranks well at all.
- **DECIDED (same session):** operator walked §8 — **all six recommended options approved** (shape as designed w/ manual F2 trainer; pure-Python IRLS; upgrade prior_promotion slot; F3 criterion defaults; floor K=25/2 slots/≤10%; refit children keep-all). **F1+F2 greenlit — F1 build next; F3 stays double-gated (criterion + go).** Watches unchanged from D131 (first v17 batches; new-arm draws — tonight's EOD read #1 doubles as this design's motivating evidence check; em tiny-n weight).

## Current status — 2026-06-08 (D110 — aged-out watermark recalibration; §7.3 wedge diagnosed + fixed + DEPLOYED LIVE — SUPERSEDES below)

**Diagnosed + fixed a §7.3 limiter wedge that had stalled ALL generation for ~17h** (758 consecutive blocked iterations 06-08 00:01→15:37; **0 submissions** under v10/v11/v12). The D109 "clean cutover / limiter held `ba92e7ee` 0/200" note below was masking a *permanent* wedge, not a benign hold.

- **Root cause (Decision D110):** D052's aged-out flush watermark is `min(decided_at)` over Crucible's gated-runs export — valid only for a shallow window (top-1000 at D052). The publisher now exports **top-10000**, and the 2026-06-07 re-gate spike (~7,056 decisions in one day = 70% of the window) compressed its time-span to ~13 days, dragging `min(decided_at)` up to 2026-05-26 — just below the entire May-29+ backlog. The flush no-op'd; the limiter pinned on the oldest `submitted` batch (`ba92e7ee`, 2026-05-29, 0/200) forever. **274 of 279 in-flight batches stranded (48,198 rows).** Same *symptom* as D061, different *mechanism* (D061 = tzinfo coercion; D110 = watermark calibration under a window shape D052 didn't anticipate).
- **Fix:** watermark → `max(decided_at) − STRANDED_AFTER` (`timedelta(days=8)`) — tracks Crucible's decision clock minus a latency margin; immune to window-span compression. Dual guard (config_hash NOT IN export) + D061 naive-watermark retained. `max` not `utc_now()` → flush freezes safely if Crucible dies. 8d > observed p99 (~7.2d, window-biased high) → cannot flush legit-pending; tunable DOWN later.
- **Verification (TDD RED→GREEN):** 2 new consumer tests (deep/compressed-window flush; margin safety boundary) + 2 existing D052 tests re-dated beyond the margin + helper `decided_at` param. 24/24 consumer; **517 passed** across feedback/cli/submission/integration/invariants. ruff check + mypy strict clean on changed scope (test file left in the repo's pre-existing hand-format per the pre-commit-bypass history).
- **DEPLOYED + VERIFIED LIVE 2026-06-08 16:52 PDT:** restarted `forge.service` (D110 via editable install). First reconcile flushed **16,404 dead rows / 82 batches** (in-flight 279→197; `submitted` 48,102→31,698), advancing the limiter off `ba92e7ee` → now `ec4c6592` (05-31). Matched the read-only dry-run exactly.
- **Recovery: ACCELERATED (operator chose immediate full recovery).** After the D110 deploy, a one-time sentinel flush (mirrors `_flush_aged_out_submissions`: `status='gated'` + nil-UUID, all `submitted` rows NOT in the current export — all dead: 0% gated, not in export, Crucible inbox drained) cleared the remaining **31,419 rows / all in-flight batches** → `submitted`=0, in-flight=0 (status now gated=92,189 / skipped=200). Limiter fully clear → **v12 generation RESUMED** (first time since 06-07 22:41). Enumeration confirmed live: `rank_combiner_share event_momentum/mean_reversion/trend_continuation=0.33` (H1 ON) + `event_momentum` in hypothesis_weights (H2 ON).

NOT an outage. **v12 throughput is Crucible-capacity-bound:** Forge over-produced ~92K vs Crucible's few-k/day; once each new v12 batch is in flight the limiter again (correctly) throttles until Crucible gates ≥80% — needs Crucible capacity or reduced Forge cadence (D070 67× mismatch), not a Forge change.

## Current status — 2026-06-08 (D109 — H1 cross_sectional_rank + H2 event_momentum, grammar v11→v12 — DEPLOYED + VERIFIED LIVE — SUPERSEDES below)

**The two contracts-gated arms of the new-hypotheses program shipped together as v12 (Decision D109)**, now that Crucible landed + verified all the upstream on 2026-06-08 (contracts 1.16.0 rank fields + `event_momentum`/`post_event_drift` literals; the `cross_sectional_rank_composable` runner; `event_momentum` dispatch; the `days_since_earnings`→`calendar` reclassification; Polygon EPS ingest — confirmed by Crucible's `FORGE_days_since_earnings_family_response.md`). **Operator chose to bundle H1+H2 into one v12 bump** (AskUserQuestion 2026-06-08), since §2.1 resolved before H1 shipped (the spec's sanctioned fallback). Spec: `H1_H2_V12_IMPLEMENTATION_SPEC.md`.

- **H1 — cross_sectional_rank combiner (the breadth lever).** New `RANK_COMBINER_HYPOTHESES = {trend_continuation, mean_reversion, event_momentum}`. `sample_config` gains `rank_combiner_share`: an eligible hypothesis draws a rank combiner (`rank_k∈{5,10,20}`, `rebalance∈{weekly,monthly}`, `direction_mode∈{long_only,long_short}`, `underlying=None`) with that probability, **drawn LAST + gated so the cold/`{}`/0.0 paths stay byte-identical** (hard rule #6). Trade count becomes deterministic (`~rank_k×rebalances ≫ 100`). **Load-bearing fix:** `ExpectedTradesFilter` gets a structural branch for rank configs (`directions×rank_k×rebalances`) so they're NOT killed on the stale single-name prior — the whole point of the combiner. Routes to Crucible's composable runner by `combiner.type` (verified `runner.py:602-607`; no `top_n` stamp needed). **Rank emission is ON by default** at the ~1/3 share (the point of v12; "like every prior weight addition" — sampler-core no-share path stays byte-identical for tests/determinism, #6); `--no-cross-sectional-rank` is an operational kill switch (operator review flipped this from the initial flag-OFF — H4 was a quality lever, H1 is the breadth lever).
- **H2 — event_momentum / PEAD (new directional hypothesis, always-on in v12).** directional `sue` (post_event_drift surprise → drift), timing gate `days_since_earnings` (calendar post-§2.1, op "<" {3..10}td "fire after the print" → orthogonal to the vol_event IV-crush sleeves), drift-decay `time_stop` + optional momentum trailing, no `hard_profit_target` (convex). Horizon-matched DTE (sue 10td → swing_short/mid), single-name (ETF-excluded — `days_since_earnings` sentinels on ETFs). The regime requirement is folded into `_build_regime_pool` (sampler-side policy, **NOT a 22nd grammar rule** — hard rule #1). §2.4: its productive form is cross-sectional → it is also rank-eligible.
- **Why v12 has NO `rules:` text change (hard rule #1):** S1 is a `cardinality` rule (count==1); the allowed hypotheses live in the contracts Literal (1.16.0 adds event_momentum) and the combiner type in `CombinerSpec` — neither is in the rules text. Pure Python-side enumeration policy (the v5–v11 pattern). `len(grammar.rules) == 21` unchanged; `v12.yaml` archived byte-identical (loader consistency verified).

## Current status — 2026-06-08 (D108 — H4 orthogonal-yield discount — BUILT + VERIFIED, flag-OFF byte-identical, deploy + flag-flip operator-gated — SUPERSEDES below)

**The versionless H4 arm of the new-hypotheses program is built (Decision D108; no grammar bump).** New `rejection_weights.compute_orthogonal_yield_discounts` discounts each **(hypothesis, directional, underlying-NAME)** factor cell's underlying-draw weight by the Grinold marginal-value factor `max(min_discount, (1+m)**-strength)` (m = the cell's version-weighted component count), so an over-mined name (the 37th AAPL long-vol clone, marginal portfolio value ≈ 0 per the pod-shop model) yields draw probability to its minting peers. Reuses the D105/D106 engine (`_component_rate_sums`, `tiebreak_weight=0.0` for a pure component count → anti-Goodhart: keys on components, never trades; m=0 cells absent → discount 1.0). Attaches at `_pick_underlying` (the only draw where the cell is fully determined), multiplying the existing D105/D106 weight BEFORE the exploration floor. Behind an **A/B flag** `forge run --orthogonal-yield` (default OFF → loader skipped, sampler gets `{}`, byte-identical; hard rule #6, pinned by a phase-2 invariant + 2 sampler cold-start tests).

- **NAME granularity — deviation from this file's earlier "class" scoping, operator-approved (AskUserQuestion 2026-06-08).** The required emission proof (live export ⋈ a read-only `forge.db` snapshot — the service held the live lock, so I analysed a checkpointed copy) showed the **class**-level cell FAILS the goal: H4 only redistributes *within* a fixed (hyp, dir), and at class granularity the only move is high_idio→diversified — but diversified vol_event mints ~0.7% vs high_idio's 6.1%, so a class discount **dilutes raw yield, doesn't orthogonalise**. The "36-on-AAPL" concentration is per-NAME inside high_idio; the name cell spreads AAPL→NVDA/AMD (~20% more name-concentration cut per unit yield cost; the `(1+m)**-s` curve self-limits so only AAPL-grade cells bite). Natural D105(class)→D106(name) progression. **Honest ceiling (told the operator):** H4 can't touch the dominant FACTOR (variance-risk-premium) concentration all high_idio vol_event components share — that needs hyp/dir diversification (H1/H2), not the underlying draw. H4 is a QUALITY lever against a BREADTH-bound gate → modest by construction.
- **Calibration (operator-chosen, AskUserQuestion):** `strength=0.15` (gentle — top live cell AAPL m≈20 → 0.64 discount; steady-state first-order name-concentration -13% at raw-yield -17%, honouring "yield roughly flat"), `min_discount=0.25` (hard cap; the 0.01 underlying floor is the second guard). Pure-√ (0.5) confirmed too aggressive (~46% raw-yield cut). Both are module constants; the flag is the switch, the strength the dial.
- **Verification:** RED→GREEN TDD — new `test_orthogonal_yield.py` (12: exact Grinold math, anti-Goodhart absent-cell, monotone, cell-separates-directional+name, min_discount cap, strength-0-disables, relative_value-no-cell, version downweight, cold-start drop, determinism) + 5 new sampler tests (cold-start byte-identical ×2, tilts-from-crowded-name mass-preserved, floor-preserved, slices-by-hyp-dir-in-sample_config) + new flag-off invariant. **Full unit suite 1,215/0; feedback+enumeration+cli+invariants 640/0; ruff + format clean; mypy --strict clean (80 files).** Built in the LIVE tree (like D107) but the flag-OFF-byte-identical property IS the D104 guard — the systemd unit has no `--orthogonal-yield`, so a reboot on the uncommitted code changes nothing.
- **OWED (operator-gated, two clean steps):** (1) stop service → full uncontended suite → commit D108 → restart (still byte-identical, code landed); (2) add `--orthogonal-yield` to the `forge.service` unit + restart when ready to A/B. **Attribution:** versionless, so read it in the submission mix — watch the `orthogonal_yield_discounts:` journal line (most-mined cells) and the realized-component name-Herfindahl falling at ~flat component-rate (the success metric, not raw count). Tree is DIRTY (H4 uncommitted + STATUS/D108 docs) — commit recommended.
- **Next program increments:** H1 (cross-sectional rank) + H2 (event-momentum) remain contracts-gated — flip enumeration on (versionless) when Crucible lands the runner + indicators (`PROMPT_CRUCIBLE_NEW_HYPOTHESES_V11.md`, sent 2026-06-07).

## Current status — 2026-06-08 (D107 — v11 H3 dealer-gamma regime switch, BOTH SIDES — DEPLOYED + VERIFIED LIVE — SUPERSEDES below)

**Update (same session): the mean_reversion side was folded into v11** (the complete switch). Both sides live-proven on the running service: `TREND regime pool = (adx, gamma_flip_distance_pct, hurst, rv_rank)` (~25% gamma when uniform); `MEAN_REV regime pool = (gamma_flip_distance_pct, iv_rank)` (~50% gamma). R1 now accepts `gamma_flip_distance_pct` (alternative to iv_rank); `_regime_signal_params` flips the gamma op to `"<"` for MR (long-gamma/ranging) vs `">"` for trend (short-gamma/amplifying). Folded (not a v12 bump) because the §7.3 rate limiter held ALL v11 submissions → 0 cohort, no attribution cost. **Full suite 1,348 / 0** (5 new MR-gamma TDD tests); ruff + mypy clean; restarted 2026-06-08 05:01 PDT, `grammar_version=v11`, 0 GrammarVersionError. **The "1/2" question (operator):** declined a static trend up-weight — completing the MR side raises gamma's footprint ~2.5%→~6.5% of draws organically; the read-speed bottleneck is Crucible's queue, not the gamma share; a feedback-driven boost is the principled path post-first-data. **Promise: modest** (gamma_flip 0-for-everything-so-far; a quality lever against a breadth-bound gate → structurally low ceiling vs H1/H2). **Committed `14864d5` on main (D107/v11, 14 files, tree clean); D104 window closed.** (Pre-commit needed `--no-verify`: this sandbox shell lacks `python` on PATH for the two grammar hooks — they pass via venv, exit 0 — and `uv run` re-touches `uv.lock`; all checks ran green manually: ruff, mypy 0/80, both grammar hooks, suite 1348/0.)

**H4 SCOPED (next focused unit) — orthogonal-yield via a √Breadth marginal-value discount.** Locus: `forge.feedback.rejection_weights` (`_component_rate_posteriors` / the compute_*_weights family), NOT the diversifier (which is selection-side + Jaccard-blind to factor cells — two AAPL long-vol configs with different thresholds have low Jaccard but identical factor cell). Design: discount each cell's component-rate weight by `1/sqrt(1 + component_count(cell))` (Grinold/pod-shop: the m-th correlated sleeve ≈ 1/√m of the first), keyed on the factor cell (hypothesis × directional × underlying-class), exploration floor preserved, behind an A/B flag (hard rule #6 — off = byte-identical). Anti-Goodhart by construction: discount = 1.0 at count 0 (dead cells stay low via their rate; not inflated), bites only over-represented cells. Reuses existing plumbing + data (`_component_rate_sums` already holds `[total, component_count]`). **Open calibration (why it's a focused unit, not a tail-of-session rush):** `1/√(1+37)≈0.16` is a 6× cut to the top cell — too aggressive for "yield roughly flat"; the discount curve needs an emission proof against the live export + D105-style anti-Goodhart regression tests before ship. **Owed:** implement with emission proof; then versionless deploy (restart, no grammar bump). Original trend-side block below.

**DEPLOYED + VERIFIED 2026-06-03 ~08:44 PDT.** Crucible confirmed BOTH percentile paths live (`494cf96` strategy path + `3c57fb4` feature-cache writer — the second gate that would otherwise make `signal_density` reject every percentile config). Deploy ritual: service stopped (DB lock released) → **full uncontended suite 1239 passed** → committed `f5710b9` to `main` (both grammar pre-commit hooks pass on the staged v6 bump) → `forge.service` restarted onto v6. **Verified live (loop iteration 317):** `grammar_version=v6`, registry loaded from export, `NRestarts=0`, `ExecMainStatus=0`, no traceback/SchemaVersionMismatch, reconciled 166 batches. **Emission verified on the deployed code** (standalone sample, real registry): 661 mean_reversion directional signals percentile-emitting (example `rsi_14 → {threshold:0.0821, op:"<", use_percentile:True, percentile_window:252}`), **0 of 2843 `volatility_event` signals carry `use_percentile`** (the untouched-archetype invariant holds live). **Owed:** tell the Crucible agent v6 is live so it can run `crucible funnel --compare v5 v6` once a v6 cohort gates (first v6 batch submits after iteration 317's ~38-min prefetch). **Minor funnel-hygiene note:** each batch re-reads `grammar.yaml`, so the v6 *version string* went live on the old (absolute) code the moment the YAML was edited — a brief v6-stamped-but-absolute window before the code restart; the service was stopped ~7 min into iteration 317's prefetch so almost certainly 0 such configs submitted. Decision **D099**.

---

**Worked the operator's "Grammar A" dispatch (percentile-parameterize signal thresholds) into a verified plan + a Crucible handoff (then built + deployed v6).** Decision **D099**.

**Why:** the binding constraint on discovery is signal **firing** (~70% of decided runs never trade; mean_reversion ~78% directional-never-fired = absolute `rsi_2` too tight; trend_continuation ~58% regime-gated). Root cause is Forge-side: the sampler draws **absolute** thresholds via `rng.uniform` over once-on-SPY ranges (`indicator_thresholds.py:419,425`) → uncontrolled per-name fire rate. Fix: emit a **percentile of the indicator's own trailing distribution** for raw-unit indicators.

**Two dispatch premises checked before acting** (one false, one I mis-scoped — both load-bearing): (1) `SignalSpec` has **no** `use_percentile`/`percentile_window` fields — it's a generic `params: dict` (contracts 1.14.0), so percentile params ride as opaque keys (**no contracts change**) and Crucible must *learn* to read them; the named fields live in a *different, unwired* Crucible class off the SignalSpec path. (2) The cited mandate "DESIGN.md §5.2:613 (`RSI below the 5th percentile…`)" points at **Crucible's** DESIGN.md §5.2 (verbatim there: "prefer percentile-based thresholds over absolute"), which I'd mis-scoped to *Forge's* DESIGN.md where it's genuinely absent. So percentile thresholds are a **pre-existing pipeline requirement** (Crucible §5.2 + base signal) — new only in Forge's *enumeration layer* + the SignalSpec/strategy-path wiring (Crucible `494cf96`). Also: Crucible's strategy-path would **misroute** unknown percentile keys into indicator construction (`if k not in ("threshold","op")`), so emitting before Crucible handles them is harmful, not inert → Crucible-first is mandatory.

**Operator decisions (AskUserQuestion 2026-06-02):** Build order = **Crucible-first handoff** (Forge doesn't edit Crucible; lands its v6 emission only after Crucible confirms). Scope = **binding-constraint only** — mean_reversion directional pool (C2 `mean_reversion ∪ dealer_positioning`) + trend_continuation `adx`/`hurst` gate; **exclude** `rv_rank`/`iv_rank` (already-rank), `days_to_*`/vol indicators, pairs, and the whole `volatility_event` archetype (fires fine). Params = **[0,1] percentile** `{threshold:<0..1>, op, use_percentile:true, percentile_window:252}`.

**Shipped this turn (docs/handoff only):** `PROMPT_CRUCIBLE_PERCENTILE_THRESHOLDS.md` (the Crucible handoff — exact change path verified feasible/low-risk: `compute()` already yields a full series, `_indicator_cache` already builds then discards it, `_percentile_rank` already exists, `underlying_bars()` is `<=asof` so no lookahead; includes the param-exclusion fix + insufficient-history guard + backward-compat + no-op-until-Forge-emits); D099 decision entry; `docs/DESIGN.md` §5.3.3 amendment.

**Crucible LANDED (2026-06-02, commit `494cf96`)** — contract implemented **verbatim** (`{threshold∈[0,1], op, use_percentile:true, percentile_window:int=252}`), both roles, warmup→safe default, no-op on raw traffic, no contracts/gate change. **Committed but NOT yet deployed** — goes live on the next crucible-runner restart (bundled with firing-summary/007); Crucible confirms "deployed + verified no-op on live raw traffic" after. **Forge deploy-gate:** may BUILD v6 now (contract locked → zero rework risk) but does **not** flip live emission until Crucible's runner is confirmed deployed on percentile-mode (else keys misroute on the raw runner). **Operator (2026-06-02):** build v6 now (deploy-gated); scope = exclude dealer_positioning directional (percentile-ize `mean_reversion`-family directional `rsi_2`/`rsi_14` + `adx`/`hurst` regime; rv_rank/iv_rank/dealer_positioning/volatility_event untouched). **SECOND GATE FOUND:** Crucible's feature-cache WRITER (`optbt/persistence/feature_cache.py`) is a separate still-absolute path `494cf96` didn't touch — Forge's pre-filters query it, so without a writer percentile fix `signal_density` rejects every percentile config (v6 worse than v5). **Deploy now DOUBLE-GATED** (strategy-path `494cf96` + feature-cache writer). Second handoff: `PROMPT_CRUCIBLE_PERCENTILE_FEATURE_CACHE.md`. Forge build scope grows to include `SyntheticFeatureCache` percentile-awareness (faithful battery tests).

**Forge v6 emission BUILT + verified 2026-06-02** (committed `f5710b9` + deployed 2026-06-03 — see deploy note at top of block). Landed as one unit: (1) `indicator_thresholds.py` — add `directional_percentile_range`/`regime_percentile_range` to `IndicatorThresholdSpec`, populate in-scope (rsi_2/rsi_14/rsi/zscore_returns/bb_pct directional; adx/hurst regime), `sample_threshold_params` emits `{threshold∈[0,1], op, use_percentile:true, percentile_window:252}` for those, **bypassing** the native-unit auto-tightening `_effective_range` (D073 reconcile); add `is_percentile_emitting(indicator,role)` helper. (2) `prefilters/feature_cache.py` (SyntheticFeatureCache) — percentile-aware activations. (3) `feedback/threshold_proposer.py` — skip percentile-eligible pairs (native tightenings incoherent for them). (4) tests: emission unit + sampler invariant (volatility_event never carries use_percentile; mean_reversion rsi_2 does) + determinism (#6) + threshold∈[0,1]. (5) grammar `v5→v6` (archive + hook; no `rules:` text change per D098). (6) docs: `INDICATOR_THRESHOLDS.md` percentile section + D099. **Verification:** new `test_percentile_thresholds.py` (RED→GREEN, incl. volatility_event-never-percentile + mean_reversion-rsi-does invariants) + **full unit suite 1090 passed** + 336 enumeration/invariants/grammar/feedback; ruff + format + mypy --strict clean on changed scope; `v6.yaml` byte-identical + loader/doc-sync pass. **OQ filed Q26** (hurst regime op `<` looks backwards for trend_continuation; v6 preserves op + still loosens — flip is a separate operator decision). **Decision:** D099 (built+verified addendum). **To deploy:** Crucible confirms BOTH percentile paths live → operator full-suite-uncontended gate → commit D099+v6 → `forge.service` restart onto v6 → `crucible funnel --compare v5 v6`.

**Deferred Forge build (its own Decision + v6 bump, AFTER Crucible confirms):** percentile emission in `indicator_thresholds.py`+`sampler.py`; TDD; deterministic seeded sampling preserved (#6); `v5→v6` (archive + hook, no `rules:` text change per D098 precedent). **Open build-time points (do NOT resolve now):** per-`(indicator,role,hypothesis)` granularity (dealer_positioning directional is shared with volatility_event; adx/hurst regime can serve relative_value); **D073 auto-tightening reconciliation** (native-unit tightenings can't apply to percentile params — needs a percentile-aware branch/skip); per-direction percentile target ranges + new `INDICATOR_THRESHOLDS.md` provenance; verify pairs/rv_rank param-merge paths stay out of the percentile set. Full detail in D099.

Each re-derived against Forge's own code first; the two non-obvious tensions escalated via AskUserQuestion and resolved by the operator.

- **Item 1 (drop `regime_arbitrage`) — SHIPPED.** New `DISABLED_HYPOTHESES` + `NON_ENUMERABLE_HYPOTHESES` (= overlay-only ∪ disabled) in `search_space.py`; the 3 enumeration-path filter sites (iterator/sampler/CLI weight-floor) read the union. **Forge runtime policy, mirroring D066's tail_hedge precedent — NOT a grammar-rule edit** (hard rule #1: the 21 rules are untouched, S1 still lists all 6). regime_arbitrage now renders with the `*` prior marker like tail_hedge.
- **Item 2 (relative_value fair test) — SHIPPED.** (2a) `_pick_underlying` returns `None` for relative_value — **reverts D079** (operator: "revert → null"; Crucible 4f5271f now loads all 37 pair legs regardless of tier, retiring the single-ticker anchor). (2b) New `cold_start_hypotheses` on `compute_trade_rate_priors` + `COLD_START_HYPOTHESES={relative_value}` policy const — **drops** poisoned prior-version gated rows entirely (vs D081's down-weight-but-keep) so the stale ~100%-zero-trade `(relative_value,*,pairs)` bucket falls below `min_bucket_samples` and the filter cold-starts to the activations heuristic. **This is the load-bearing fix**: live journal confirmed relative_value was 100% killed at `expected_trades`, 0 submitted, every batch under v4.
- **Item 4 (v4→v5 bump) — SHIPPED.** First version bump with **no `rules:` text change** (item-1/2 are enumeration-policy + pre-filter, not rule edits). `grammar.yaml` version+comment; `grammar_archive/v5.yaml` byte-identical; both grammar pre-commit hooks pass; GRAMMAR.md untouched (documents the unchanged rules; tail_hedge precedent keeps runtime policy out of it).
- **Items 5/6 — already live (D096/D097).** Stamping + funnel read the version dynamically → v5 flows with no code change. Integration test now asserts `grammar_version == "v5"`.

**Verification:** **full uncontended suite 1229 passed / 0 failed** (service stopped to release the DuckDB lock) + 6 new D098 tests + 2 new phase2 invariants; ruff + ruff format clean on changed scope; mypy --strict clean on the full 78-file `src/` tree; both grammar pre-commit hooks pass on the staged v5 bump. Also fixed a pre-existing test bug in passing: `test_hook_scripts.py` used bare `python` (absent from this host's PATH) → switched to `sys.executable`, repairing 13 long-failing hook-script tests (proven D098-independent by stash-to-HEAD). E2E smoke: 5000 configs across exactly 4 hypotheses (no regime_arbitrage/tail_hedge), relative_value ~24% with `underlying=None`, 0 invalid. (NB: this session had severe inline-stdout corruption — all gates were re-verified by capturing output to files and reading them.)

**DEPLOYED + VERIFIED 2026-05-31 ~13:28 PDT.** Service stopped → full uncontended suite **1229 passed / 0 failed** → committed `89b2bbd` on branch `v5-grammar-regime-arbitrage-drop` → `forge.service` restarted onto v5. **Verified live (loop iteration 263):** `grammar_version=v5`; `grammar_versions: recorded manual_bump row for v5` (hard rule #10 audit row auto-written + resets the D035 stuck-state streak so the 111-batch v4 zero-promotion history doesn't carry forward); `hypothesis_weights: ... regime_arbitrage=0.091* ...` — regime_arbitrage now renders as an inactive `*` prior (was a live `0.516` weight under v4 ~30 min prior), i.e. dropped from the sampler exactly as designed; `relative_value=0.091` (no `*` — has gated data, now in the active sampling set, expected_trades cold-start will let it flow). NRestarts=0, no traceback / no SchemaVersionMismatch, reconciled 112 batches. No contracts/schema change. **Still OWED: tell the Crucible agent the new grammar version string is `v5`** so it can run `crucible funnel --compare v4 v5`. Decision Log: **D098**. **Reality check (operator):** v5 is a clean baseline + relative_value test, NOT a promotion expectation — gate is full-history-honest; don't tune toward recent windows (recency-fit trap).

## Current status — 2026-05-29 (funnel instrumentation complement — SUPERSEDES below)

**Shipped D096 — the Forge-side funnel instrumentation complement (`FUNNEL_INSTRUMENTATION_FORGE.md`), restart-pending.** Completes the two upstream stages of the combined pipeline funnel (`FUNNEL_INSTRUMENTATION.md`, Crucible-side) so grammar iteration becomes measurable across the one major stage that lives only in Forge — the pre-filter battery. Operator decisions this session: Part A path = **"ship the interim join-map now + propose the durable contracts change"**; export location = **Forge's own `~/forge_data/exports/`**; and **align explicitly with the Crucible funnel spec**.

- **Part A (grammar-version slicing).** Forge cannot stamp `grammar_version` into the submission today (`submit_candidate` writes the bare `StrategyConfig`; the model is `extra="forbid"` with no such field — a real contracts gap). **Interim, Forge-only:** publish a `config_hash → grammar_version` join-map (`forge_submission_versions.json`); Crucible joins `runs.config_hash` against it for its funnel Stage 0. Well-defined via hard rule #9 (each hash submits once). **Durable:** `CONTRACTS_GRAMMAR_VERSION_PROPOSAL.md` — add `grammar_version` to the payload, **excluded from `config_hash`** so all historical hashes/joins stay valid (matches Crucible's Stage 0 as written). Join-map is the bridge until it lands.
- **Part B (pre-filter funnel export).** New `forge.funnel` subpackage aggregates `batch_summaries` per grammar version → the two Crucible `[Forge-opt]` stages (`enumerated`, `survived_prefilters`) + `rejection_breakdown` (which filter killed the rest) + `enumerated_by_hypothesis` (which branch). Written atomically to `~/forge_data/exports/forge_funnel.json` after each batch. JSON keys match the dispatch schema exactly so Crucible lines up its stages; `schema_version` self-describing (their degrade-gracefully rule).
- **Schema:** 3 idempotent ALTERs on `batch_summaries` — `enumerated_count`, `survived_count`, `enumerated_by_hypothesis` (D062/D076/D085 pattern). `batch_size` stays the post-diversifier submitted count; these add the two stages above it. Pre-D096 batches keep NULL and are excluded from the export (with a `coverage` count — no silent truncation), so the funnel invariant `sum(rejection_breakdown) == enumerated − survived` holds exactly (locked at recording + aggregation layers).
- **Tests (TDD):** 17 new funnel tests + 1 recording invariant + 1 full-run integration; **238 changed-scope + regression green** (funnel/submission/invariants/cli/persistence); ruff + mypy --strict clean. Hard-rule-#8 clock/RNG invariant scans pass with the new module (uses blessed `utc_now()`).
- **Wiring:** export refresh sits inside the per-batch submit block and is **strictly non-fatal** (failure logs + iteration proceeds; Crucible degrades gracefully without it).
- **Handoffs written:** `CRUCIBLE_FUNNEL_FORGE_HANDOFF.md` (consume both files; Stage-0 interim join-map + the divergence from their spec, resolved by the proposal), `CONTRACTS_GRAMMAR_VERSION_PROPOSAL.md`.

**Pending:** (a) operator-directed full-suite gate → commit → `forge.service` restart (no grammar/contracts/schema-breaking change; DB picks up columns on open); (b) notify the Crucible agent to wire their funnel to both files + confirm the read path; (c) operator + Crucible decision on the durable contracts proposal. Decision Log: **D096**.
